# Shade - Spirits (this class wears no symbiants)

A SHADE WEARS NO SYMBIANTS AND NO IMPLANTS. In all 13 implant slots it wears SPIRITS instead - the client's own ItemClass 5, 844 templates, 842 of them ToWear-locked to Profession 15. Verified from the data both ways: running the symbiant extractor with --prof 15 returns 14 usable symbiant templates out of 1,462 wearable ones, and 12 of those 14 are the known unlocked Xan Artillery-unit anomaly (ids 278908-278919, which carry no profession lock at all, unlike every other Artillery item) plus two unlocked one-offs (245306 Brumal Chest Symbiant of Endurance, 245895 Head Symbiant of the Love Child). There is no Shade symbiant line to pick. Spirits differ from symbiants in three ways that change how you play: they need NO surgery clinic (their ToWear carries no Treatment requirement at all - only Level, Agility, Sense, Profession and the expansion bit), they CANNOT be over-equipped, and a Spirit buffs more skills than an implant of the same QL. You get ordinary Spirits with the Spirit Siphon nano (id 297342): bring a target below 20% health, cast it, kill the target, loot the Soul Capsule, open it. The tier prefix (Bitter ... Wistful) is a QL LABEL, not a drop location - the 34-step ladder runs QL1/level 1 to QL300/level 219 and you farm a tier by siphoning mobs around that level, not by camping a named boss. Endgame is the Legacy-of-the-Xan ladder: Xan Beta Spirits (QL250 only) drop in the three LoX encounters, and you CRAFT the Alpha from a high-QL ordinary Spirit plus the matching Beta, then combine two Alphas of the same slot into a Combined Alpha. See farmingRuns for the location-by-location plan and acquisitionBySlot for the per-slot ladder.

## Why this file is called `shade-symbiants.json`

The Shade is the one profession in Anarchy Online whose implant slots do not take implants or symbiants. The client states the reason in its own item text.

- items.ocp / tools/mp-symbiant-extractor --prof 15: 120,842 templates read, 1,464 match "Symbiant", 1,462 are wearable, and only 14 come back usable by profession 15.
- Twelve of those 14 are ids 278908-278919, the Xan [slot] Symbiant, Artillery Unit set - they carry an EMPTY profession lock in items.ocp while every other Artillery symbiant carries the Soldier/Fixer/Agent/Adventurer/Trader lock. That is a client-data anomaly, not a Shade permission.
- The other two are 245306 Brumal Chest Symbiant of Endurance and 245895 Head Symbiant of the Love Child, also unlocked one-offs.
- items.ocp id 218576 "Whispering Spirit", client description: "...put it into the Right-Arm IMPLANT slot. Note: Shades can not use implants, as all the other professions. Their physique is to fragile for them to survive mechanical pieces inside. Instead the Shades of the World use captured spirits. The Spirits can be installed and taken out without the aid of any machinery. The drawback of this is that they do not accept to be overequipped."
- items.ocp filtered on ItemClass == 5 with --prof 15: 844 templates, ALL usable, 842 explicitly ToWear-locked to Shade. The other two are 215259 "Shade Implant Test item" (a development leftover) and 305506 "Ethereal Embrace".
- Regular implants are ItemClass 3: 44,060 of them exist in items.ocp and NOT ONE carries a profession lock, so the Shade prohibition is a client/server rule rather than an item criterion - which is exactly why it has to be stated on the page.
- ao-universe: "Unlike the other 13 professions... Shades cannot utilize standard implants"; wiki.aodb.us/wiki/Shade: "In their place, you will find trapped Spirits".
- Requirement shape, read from the data: Xan Right Limb Spirit of Power - Alpha (id 279217) ToWear = Expansion BitAnd 128 (Legacy of the Xan), Level > 218, Agility > 1275, Sense > 1275, Profession EqualTo 15. NO Treatment requirement - which is the mechanical difference from every symbiant and every implant in the game.

|---|---|
| Spirit templates in the client (ItemClass 5) | 844 |
| Usable by a Shade | 844 |
| With a slot | 844 |
| Ordinary tiers (Bitter..Wistful) | 34 |
| With a cited acquisition | 774 |
| Left `unknown` on purpose | 70 |

## Deliberately unverified

- No formula ties siphoned Spirit QL to mob level. The rule the sources actually state is "kill monsters whose level is around the QL you want your Spirits to be". Treat the per-tier camps as level-band guidance.
- The chance that a Soul Capsule actually contains a Spirit is not documented. A patch note records "Soul Capsules will have a higher chance of having a Spirit inside" as a past fix; a player estimate of 25-50% exists but is not an official figure and is not asserted here.
- The tradeskill and skill level required to perform an Alpha combine are UNKNOWN - the ao-universe recipe page names neither.
- Whispering Spirit (id 218576): no page on ao-universe.com, wiki.aodb.us or forums.funcom.com documents it. Its acquisition rests entirely on the client's own description text ("given to you by the Shades of the Independent Professionals Society in Jobe"). Do not confuse it with "Whispering Visitant" (an Adonis mob) or "Whispering Soul Stone" (a Jobe Research Training Facility drop).
- Ethereal Embrace (id 305506) and Ethereal Spirit of Berserker Rage (id 260763): no allowed source documents either. The allowed sources do document an "Ethereal Implant of Berserker Rage" from the Inferno quest line, but that is a symbiant and nothing connects the two. Left unknown.
- The Fabricated and Prototype Spirit families (34 templates each): the allowed sources document only the SYMBIANT counterparts (cash-shop Explorers' Packs and the symbiant Daily Reward respectively). Nothing states the Spirit versions share those sources, so they are left unknown.
- Spirit Siphon's cooldown length and its stated Dimach-skill scaling are not quantified anywhere on the allowed sources.
- 215259 "Shade Implant Test item" is a development leftover with no profession lock and no description. It is carried in the catalogue for completeness and marked not obtainable.
- The best-in-slot rows are the QL300 Combined Alphas. Every Shade below level 219 / Agility 1276 / Sense 1276 wears something lower - use the tier ladder in catalog.tiers, which carries the exact level and ability requirement of every step.
